Method: 机械方面：增加两个推进器并采用 CFD 优化外壳以提升俯仰稳定性；电气方面：修复累积的故障连接点、升级推进器控制硬件、重新设计声学系统（定制PCB+高阶滤波器）；软件方面：改进状态估计、声纳物体检测、视觉驱动任务规划和 IVC（智能视觉控制）；测试方面：优化有限水池时间的利用效率。

Result: 系统可靠性显著提升，能够支持更广泛的任务集，并保持竞赛所需的稳定表现。

Conclusion: 通过全面的子系统可靠性投资，团队首次尝试同时覆盖 RoboSub 全部设计目标，在扩大能力范围的同时维持了可靠的竞赛表现。

Abstract: The Duke Robotics Club presents Oogway and Crush, our AUVs for RoboSub 2026. This year's strategy expands on our previously narrowed scope, targeting all four of RoboSub's design goals for the first time: movement, vision, manipulation, and acoustic tracking. This expansion is based on sustained reliability investment across all three subsystems. Mechanically, Crush gained two additional thrusters and a CFD-optimized case, providing pitch stability. Electrically, we addressed accumulated failure points by repairing unreliable connections and upgraded thruster control hardware. We also redesigned our acoustics system, adding a new custom PCB with higher-order filters, significantly improving pinger detection reliability. On the software side, improvements to state estimation, sonar-based object detection, vision-driven task planning, and IVC enable more capable and coordinated autonomous runs. Paired with investments in our testing infrastructure to maximize our limited pool time, we can now attempt a broader set of tasks while maintaining the reliability our competition strategy demands.

</details>


### [109] [Importance Sampling and PCA for Finding Failures in Commercial Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18106)
*Hailey Warner,Duncan Eddy,Shreya Parjan,Caroline Cahilly,Harrison Delecki,Matthias Kleinstauber,Chaitanya Shinde,Jerry Lopez,Mykel J. Kochenderfer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文验证了两种稀有故障发现算法（自适应压力测试AST和基于扩散的故障采样DiFS）在商用自动驾驶卡车系统上的有效性，并提出了基于PCA的故障分类方法，将故障聚类为常见模式并恢复通用噪声轨迹，从而为故障发现到感知层面系统性诊断提供了路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前稀有故障发现方法仅在简单学术驾驶栈的仿真中验证，未证明能否推广至商用系统中使用的更稳健规划器。本文旨在填补这一空白，将两种稀有事件发现算法应用于商用自动驾驶卡车系统。

Method: 1) 自适应压力测试(AST)：使用强化学习搜索导致模拟碰撞的最可能噪声轨迹。2) 扩散基故障采样(DiFS)：训练去噪扩散模型以采样多样化的故障集。3) 基于PCA的统计分析：对故障进行主成分分析，聚类主成分并反演PCA变换以恢复通用噪声轨迹。

Result: 两种算法在合并和切入场景中均能发现传统蒙特卡洛模拟无法发现的模拟碰撞。恢复的通用噪声轨迹能够在相同和相似场景中复现故障。

Conclusion: 本文提供了从故障发现到感知层面系统性诊断的可行路径，证明了稀有故障发现算法在商用自动驾驶系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Methods for discovering rare failures in autonomous systems have so far been demonstrated almost exclusively in simulations with simple, academic driving stacks, leaving open whether they generalize to the more robust planners used in commercial systems. We address this gap by applying two rare-event discovery algorithms to a commercial autonomous trucking stack. Adaptive stress testing (AST) uses reinforcement learning to search for the most likely noise trajectories leading to a simulated collision, while diffusion-based failure sampling (DiFS) trains a denoising diffusion model to sample a diverse set of failures. We show that both algorithms find simulated collisions during merge and cut-in maneuvers where traditional Monte Carlo simulation does not. To make these failures actionable, we introduce a statistical analysis based on principal component analysis (PCA) that classifies failures into common modes and identifies the timesteps that most influence the outcome. We cluster the principal components and invert the PCA transform to recover generalized noise trajectories, and show that these trajectories reproduce failures in identical and similar scenarios. This provides a path from failure discovery to systematic diagnosis of perception-level flaws.

</details>


### [110] [Isaac Sim-to-Real: Reinforcement Learning based Locomotion for Quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18135)
*Jordan Dowdy,Jean Chagas Vaz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于强化学习的四足机器人全身控制框架，利用NVIDIA Isaac Sim和Isaac Lab进行训练，实现了零样本的sim-to-real迁移。在Unitree Go1机器人上的实验表明，该策略在速度跟踪性能上与内置控制器相当，但具有更强的抗干扰能力，并能达到2.0 m/s的线速度和1.8 rad/s的角速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习策略在仿真中表现良好，但部署到物理系统时常遇到sim-to-real差距问题，需要一种能够实现零样本迁移的鲁棒控制框架。

Method: 基于强化学习的鲁棒全身控制框架，使用NVIDIA Isaac Sim和Isaac Lab进行训练，实现零样本sim-to-real策略迁移。

Result: 在Unitree Go1四足机器人上验证，速度跟踪性能与内置控制器相当，抗大扰动能力更强，可实现2.0 m/s线速度和1.8 rad/s角速度。

Conclusion: 所提出的RL框架能够实现零样本sim-to-real迁移，在物理硬件上取得与内置控制器相当甚至更强的控制性能。

Abstract: Learning-based approaches to locomotion have risen in popularity in recent years, showing the capability for complex legged locomotion and whole-body control. Reinforcement learning (RL), the primary learning-based approach for locomotion, often utilizes a high-performance simulation tool, providing a controlled and efficient training and development environment. However, policies that perform well in simulation frequently encounter unexpected challenges when deployed on a physical system, known as the sim-to-real gap. This work presents a robust RL locomotion framework capable of whole-body control. The proposed RL framework utilizes Nvidia's new set of simulation tools, Isaac Sim, and its companion RL framework, Isaac Lab, for training, achieving a zero-shot sim-to-real policy. The performance of our policy is validated on physical hardware using the Unitree Go1, with experimental results showing similar velocity tracking performance to the quadruped's integrated controller, with a greater ability to recover from large disturbances, and achieve linear velocities of 2.0 m/s and angular velocities of 1.8 rad/s.

</details>


### [111] [World Translation: Minimizing Sim-to-Real Gap with Backward Dynamics Extraction and Unpaired Domain Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18154)
*Xinchen Yao,Leixin Chang,Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出World Translation方法，通过将不可观测的动态信息从实际观测中反向提取，并作为非配对域迁移问题在仿真与现实间传递，解决了部分可观测性导致的学习动力学模型不准确问题，在多种机器人平台上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有real-to-sim方法中的学习动力学模型面临部分可观测性问题：相同观测可能因不可观测因素导致不同转移，而现有方法假设这些因素可从历史观测中恢复，在历史不具信息量时（如突然接触事件）会失败。

Method: World Translation利用仿真器（确定性但物理不完美）和学习模型（准确但在部分可观测下欠定）的互补优势：从已观测到的转移中反向提取不可观测的动态信息，然后将此特征作为非配对域迁移问题在仿真与现实之间传递，保留动态内容的同时转换域风格。

Result: 在人体、四足和机械臂平台上，方法均实现了比基线更准确的动力学建模，在不可观测因素无法从历史观测中恢复的场景下优势最大；Go2四足机器人的真实部署验证了策略迁移的改进。

Conclusion: 通过逆向提取不可观测动态信息并将其作为域迁移问题处理，有效解决了部分可观测性下的动力学建模难题，提升了sim-to-real策略迁移效果。

Abstract: The gap between simulation and reality remains a fundamental challenge in deploying simulation-trained robotic policies in the real world. Real-to-sim methods narrow this gap from the real side, learning transition dynamics from real data to build a more realistic digital world. Learned dynamics models are their dominant instance. Such methods, however, face a partial observability problem: the same observation may branch to different transitions due to unobservable factors. Existing methods assume these factors can be recovered from observation history. However, this may fail whenever observation history is uninformative, such as a sudden contact event with no prior warning. To address this limitation, we propose \textit{World Translation}, which exploits a complementary strength of simulators and learned dynamics. Simulators are deterministic but physically imperfect, while learned models are accurate but underdetermined under partial observability. Rather than predicting transitions forward from history, we extract the unobservable dynamics information backward from an observed transition, then translate this feature across simulation and reality as an unpaired domain-translation problem that preserves dynamics content while transferring domain style. Experiments across humanoid, quadruped, and manipulator platforms show that our method achieves more accurate dynamics modeling than baselines, with the largest gains when unobservable factors cannot be recovered from observation history. Real-robot deployment on Go2 quadruped confirms improved policy transfer.

</details>


### [112] [Imitation of Arm Gestures by the Semi-Humanoid Robot NICO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18197)
*Anastasiya Ihnatovich,Igor Farkaš*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于解析几何和MediaPipe姿态估计的半人形机器人NICO手臂手势模仿系统，通过单目RGB图像实时计算人体关节角度并映射到机器人运动配置，初步实验表明该方法在简单手势上效果良好，但在复杂姿态和手腕运动上存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现人机交互中机器人对人类手臂手势的模仿能力，利用人形机器人的拟人特征提升交互自然性。

Method: 使用MediaPipe框架从单目RGB帧中提取人体3D关键点坐标（包括手臂关节和手部关键点），通过解析几何关系计算关节角度，并将角度映射到半人形机器人NICO的电机配置，执行预定义运动序列。

Result: 在六名不同身高参与者的代表性手臂手势实验中，该方法能从单目RGB输入生成有意义的模仿动作，但在复杂姿态和手腕相关运动中表现受限。

Conclusion: 基于解析几何和MediaPipe的方法能实现基本的单目RGB手势模仿，但仍需改进对复杂姿态和手部精细运动的处理能力。

Abstract: Seamless human-robot interaction (HRI) requires a number of perceptual and motor abilities from the robot, one of them being the imitation of human gestures. Humanoid robots have an advantage in HRI thanks to their anthropomorphic features. In this work, we develop a system for imitation of human arm gestures by the semi-humanoid robot NICO based on analytical geometry and a pretrained MediaPipe pose-estimation model. For each input RGB frame, 3D coordinates of relevant human body landmarks, including arm joints and hand keypoints, are obtained using the MediaPipe framework. Joint angles are then computed from these coordinates using derived geometric relations. Finally, the computed angles are properly mapped to NICO's motor configuration and executed in a predefined motion sequence. Preliminary experiments on several representative arm gestures with six participants of different height indicate that the proposed method can produce meaningful imitative motions from monocular RGB input only, while also highlighting limitations in more complex poses and wrist-related movements.

</details>


### [113] [Learning Adaptive Safety Margins for Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18200)
*Junyi Hu,Shuaihang Yuan,Geeta Chandra Raju Bethala,Anthony Tzes,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种上下文条件安全评判器（Context-Conditioned Safety Critic），通过自适应安全裕度偏好对扩散规划器生成的轨迹候选进行排序，结合三项互补损失（安全、效率、距离约束匹配），并通过两阶段师生蒸馏将特权ESDF几何知识迁移到纯感知选择器，从而在杂乱室内导航中实现更高的成功率和路径效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在杂乱室内空间中的导航失败往往不是因为无法生成无碰撞路径，而是因为固定的安全裕度校准不当——保守裕度导致绕路和超时，激进裕度在感知偏差下产生近边界捷径。现有扩散规划器能从RGB-D生成多样轨迹候选，但可靠的选择机制仍是瓶颈。

Method: 提出上下文条件安全评判器，分解为三项：①安全项（清除预算惩罚+控制障碍函数残差，保证航点和过渡安全性）；②效率项（平滑惩罚+安全门控绕路比惩罚，避免绕路但不激励风险捷径）；③距离约束匹配项（将学习预算与实际ESDF清除距离对齐，防止裕度坍缩）。在仿真中用特权ESDF几何训练评判器，通过两阶段师生蒸馏将其蒸馏为纯感知选择器。

Result: 在HM3D和MP3D的PointGoal导航（含跨数据集迁移）中，该方法在成功率和SPL上均超过强扩散基线、优化基线和RL基线。纯仿真训练后可直接迁移到Unitree G1人形机器人上，在杂乱室内场景中无需任务特定调参即可导航。

Conclusion: 学习自适应安全裕度偏好而非使用固定裕度，结合特权几何蒸馏到纯感知，可显著提升机器人在杂乱室内空间中的导航性能和泛化能力，且仿真到实物的零样本迁移效果好。

Abstract: Robots in cluttered indoor spaces often fail not because they cannot generate collision-free paths, but because a fixed safety margin is mis-calibrated: conservative margins cause detours and timeouts, while permissive margins lead to near-boundary shortcuts under perception bias. Diffusion-based planners propose diverse trajectory candidates from egocentric RGB-D, yet reliable selection remains the bottleneck. We propose a context-conditioned safety critic that learns an adaptive clearance preference for ranking diffusion proposals, decomposed into three complementary terms: (i) a safety term with a clearance-budget penalty and a control-barrier-function residual for waypoint- and transition-wise safety, (ii) an efficiency term combining a smoothness penalty with a safety-gated detour-ratio penalty that avoids detours without incentivizing risky shortcuts, and (iii) a distance-constraint matching term that anchors the learned budget to realized ESDF clearances to prevent margin collapse. We train the critic with privileged ESDF geometry in simulation and distill it into a perception-only selector via a two-stage teacher-student procedure. On PointGoal navigation in HM3D and MP3D, including cross-dataset transfer, our method achieves the highest success rate (SR) and success weighted by path length (SPL) among strong diffusion, optimization, and RL baselines. Trained purely in simulation, it transfers to a Unitree G1 humanoid and navigates cluttered indoor scenes without task-specific tuning.

</details>


### [114] [Optimization of sim-to-real transfer in the humanoid robot NICO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18210)
*Juraj Gavura,Igor Farkaš*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于触觉标定的低成本抓取流水线，通过YOLO检测、立体视觉定位和视觉反馈模型，在无需RGB-D相机或外部追踪系统的情况下，缩小了仿真到现实的差距，提高了人形机器人NICO在桌面场景中的抓取成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真中规划的动作在物理机器人上执行时存在仿真到现实的差距（sim-to-real gap），导致定位误差和抓取失败。此前工作仅改进了2D到达精度，本文将其扩展至桌面物体抓取场景。

Method: 在先前触觉标定方法基础上，引入YOLO目标检测和手部检测、基于机器人内置低分辨率鱼眼相机的立体视觉定位、以及任务特定的抓取执行校正。同时实现了视觉反馈模型，在抓取前将机器手与检测到的物体对齐。整体构成了无需RGB-D相机、动捕或外部追踪系统的标定驱动抓取流水线。

Result: 完全非线性标定模型在校准区域内表现最佳；视觉反馈模型在整个桌面工作空间内实现了最高的整体抓取成功率。

Conclusion: 所提出的标定驱动抓取流水线有效弥补了仿真到现实的差距，低成本方案在桌面抓取任务中可行且有效，其中视觉反馈模型在通用性上优于单纯标定模型。

Abstract: Robotic grasping requires accurate coordination between visual perception, object localization, inverse kinematics, and hand control. However, when movements planned in simulation are executed on a physical robot, the sim-to-real gap can cause small positioning errors that prevent successful grasping. In our previous work, we introduced a low-cost haptic calibration method that improved 2D reaching accuracy of the humanoid robot NICO. In this paper, we extend this approach from reaching to tabletop object grasping by adding YOLO-based object and hand detection, stereo vision-based localization using the robot's built-in low-resolution fisheye cameras, and task-specific corrections for grasp execution. Together, these components form a novel calibration-based grasping pipeline that does not require RGB-D cameras, motion capture, or external tracking systems. We also implemented a visual feedback model that aligns the robot hand with the detected object before grasping. Our results show that the fully nonlinear calibration model achieved the best performance inside the calibrated area, while the visual feedback model achieved the highest overall grasping success across the full tabletop workspace.

</details>


### [115] [FM-VLA: Force-based Memory for Vision-Language-Action Models in Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18231)
*Ruicheng Li,Qixiu Li,Ruichun Ma,Yu Deng,Lin Luo,Zhiying Du,Jianfeng Xiang,Huizhi Liang,Ruicheng Wang,Jiaolong Yang,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FM-VLA提出了一种基于力记忆的VLA模型，通过VAE将力历史编码为紧凑的记忆token，使得视觉-语言-动作模型能够在非马尔可夫、接触丰富的操作任务中进行时序上下文推理，相比依赖视觉记忆的方法，在减少计算开销的同时显著提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的记忆增强VLA模型主要依赖视觉记忆（如历史图像帧），但视觉记忆在时序事件视觉模糊时（如多次按压按钮的小幅度运动）存在根本性局限，且计算开销大。因此，作者提出利用力信号作为记忆模态来解决非马尔可夫、接触丰富操作中的时序上下文推理问题。

Method: 1) 使用VAE对力历史时间序列进行预训练重建，将力历史编码为紧凑的力记忆token；2) 将力潜在表示与短状态历史作为额外的条件token注入到动作专家模块中，使VLA能够利用累积的接触事件历史来指导操作。

Result: 在三个依赖记忆的任务（找隐藏块、按压按钮、擦拭盘子指定次数）上，FM-VLA在最小推理开销下实现了超过80%的成功率，显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 力记忆作为视觉记忆的补充模态，能有效解决视觉模糊场景下的时序推理问题，为接触丰富操作任务提供了一种轻量级、高效的记忆增强方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have achieved impressive generalization in robotic manipulation, and recent memory-augmented VLAs have relaxed the Markovian assumption by conditioning on past images or language summaries. Vision-based memory approaches address this by conditioning on sampled past image frames, but they are computationally expensive and fundamentally limited when temporal events are visually ambiguous, e.g., pushing a button multiple times with small movements. We propose FM-VLA, a VLA model with force-based memory, enabling temporal context reasoning for non-Markovian, contact-rich manipulation. We encode force histories into compact force memory tokens with a variational autoencoder (VAE) pretrained with force time series reconstruction. By projecting force latent representations and short state history as additional conditioning tokens to the action expert module, we enable VLAs to leverage accumulated contact event history to guide manipulation. We evaluate FM-VLA on three memory-dependent tasks, including finding a hidden block, pressing a button, and wiping a dish for a specific number of times. Our lightweight force memory achieves over 80% success rate with minimal inference overhead, significantly outperforming baseline approaches. Project page: https://qft-333.github.io/FM-VLA-Page/

</details>


### [116] [Patch Policy: Efficient Embodied Control via Dense Visual Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18236)
*Gaoyue Zhou,Zichen Jeff Cui,Ada Langford,Bowen Tan,Yann LeCun,Lerrel Pinto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Patch Policy是一种轻量级的架构扩展，通过块因果注意力掩码使基于Transformer的策略能够直接使用预训练的密集ViT patch特征，无需沉重的视觉-语言模型。在模拟和真实环境中，相比全局池化策略提升40%性能，相比OpenVLA-OFT提升18%而仅使用其0.7%的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人策略要么将观测压缩为单个全局token（损失细粒度空间细节），要么从头训练视觉骨干（放弃大规模预训练优势），而使用密集patch特征的大型视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）又过于笨重和缓慢。作者希望弥合这一差距，在保持轻量高效的同时充分利用预训练ViT的密集特征。

Method: 提出Patch Policy，一种最小化的架构扩展，核心是块因果注意力掩码（block-causal attention mask）。该掩码在保留标准策略时间因果性的同时，允许模型在每个观测中关注多个patch token及其他状态信息，从而直接消耗预训练的密集patch token而无需完整VLM的计算开销。

Result: 在4个模拟环境和3个真实世界环境套件中，相比使用最先进的全局池化表示的策略，Patch Policy实现了40%的相对改进；相比微调的OpenVLA-OFT，性能提升18%而仅使用其约0.7%的参数。

Conclusion: Patch Policy为机器人社区提供了一种简便的流水线，使其能够轻松利用视觉表示学习的持续进展，同时不影响高频反应式控制所需的训练效率和推理速度。

Abstract: Pretrained dense visual features from Vision Transformers (ViTs) are powerful yet have been underutilized in robot learning. Modern robot policies either compress each observation into a single global token, or rely on visual backbones trained from scratch, sacrificing both fine-grained spatial detail and the benefits of large-scale visual pre-training. While there exist policies that do operate on dense patch features like large vision-language-action models (VLAs), they tend to be heavy and slow, inheriting the full cost of a billion-parameter vision-language model (VLM) backbone. We close this gap with Patch Policy, a minimal architectural extension that enables transformer-based policies to consume dense pre-trained patch tokens directly without the computational overhead of a full VLM. At its core is a block-causal attention mask that preserves the temporal causality of standard policies while letting the model attend over many patch tokens per observation, alongside other state information. Patch Policy is lightweight, fast, and highly effective. Across four simulated and three real-world environment suites, our method achieves a 40% relative improvement over policies using state-of-the-art global-pooled representations. Furthermore, it surpasses fine-tuned OpenVLA-OFT by 18% while using roughly 0.7% of the parameters. We believe Patch Policy provides a pipeline for the robotics community to readily leverage continuing progress in visual representation learning, without sacrificing the training efficiency or inference speed required for high-frequency, reactive control. Videos can be viewed at https://patch-policy.github.io

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Reinforcement Learning-Guided NSGA-II Enhanced with Gray Relational Coefficient for Multi-Objective Optimization: Application to NASDAQ Portfolio Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16194)
*Zhiyuan Wang,Qinxu Ding,Ding Ding,Siying Zhu,Jing Ren,Yue Wang,Chong Hui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合强化学习(RL)和灰色关联度(GRC)的非支配排序遗传算法(NSGA-II)，即RL-NSGA-II-GRC，用于解决带约束的多目标优化问题，尤其在投资组合优化中同时最小化风险和最大化收益。该方法通过RL智能体自适应调整进化参数，并引入GRC锦标赛选择算子，在基准测试和NASDAQ投资组合应用中相比传统NSGA-II实现了约5.8%和4.4%的收敛性提升，生成了平滑且稠密的Pareto前沿面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束多目标优化方法在收敛性和Pareto前沿的多样性之间难以取得良好平衡，特别是在投资组合优化这类需要权衡多个冲突目标（如风险最小化与收益最大化）的实际金融场景中，缺乏能够自适应调整进化参数并有效引导搜索方向的综合方法。

Method: 1) 将强化学习(RL)智能体作为控制器集成到NSGA-II框架中，利用超体积、可行性和多样性等指标作为反馈信号，在线自适应调整进化参数；2) 设计基于灰色关联度(GRC)的增强型二元锦标赛选择算子，综合考虑非支配排序等级、拥挤距离和与理想参考点的接近程度，计算统一的评分来指导父代选择；3) 在Kursawe和CONSTR基准函数以及NASDAQ实际投资组合数据上进行验证。

Result: 在Kursawe和CONSTR基准测试中，RL-NSGA-II-GRC相比标准NSGA-II分别实现了约5.8%和4.4%的收敛性提升，同时保持了良好的解分布多样性。在NASDAQ投资组合应用中，生成了平滑且密集填充的有效前沿面，支持识别最大夏普比组合（年化夏普比=1.92）以及不同风险厌恶水平下的效用最优组合。

Conclusion: 提出的RL-NSGA-II-GRC方法通过RL智能体自适应控制和GRC综合指标选择有效提升了多目标优化的收敛性和Pareto前沿质量，在金融投资组合优化中能够生成稠密的有效前沿面，为决策者提供可操作的投资见解。

Abstract: In modern financial markets, decision-makers increasingly rely on quantitative methods to navigate complex trade-offs among multiple, often conflicting objectives. This paper addresses constrained multi-objective optimization (MOO) with an application to portfolio optimization for minimizing risk and maximizing return. To address existing gaps, we propose a novel reinforcement learning (RL)-guided non-dominated sorting genetic algorithm II (NSGA-II) enhanced with gray relational coefficients (GRC), termed RL-NSGA-II-GRC, which combines an RL agent controller and GRC-based selection to improve convergence and diversity of Pareto fronts. The agent adapts evolutionary parameters online using metrics of hypervolume, feasibility, and diversity, while the GRC tournament operator ranks parents via a unified score considering dominance rank, crowding distance, and proximity to ideal reference. We evaluate the framework on the Kursawe and CONSTR benchmarks and a NASDAQ portfolio application. On the benchmarks, RL-NSGA-II-GRC achieves convergence improvements of about 5.8% and 4.4% over NSGA-II, while preserving well-distributed non-dominated solutions. In the portfolio application, it produces a smooth, densely populated efficient frontier supporting identification of the maximum Sharpe ratio portfolio (annualized Sharpe =1.92) and utility-optimal portfolios for different risk-aversion levels. The main contributions are three-fold: 1) we propose an RL-NSGA-II-GRC method integrating an RL agent into the evolutionary framework to adaptively control parameters via generational feedback; 2) we design a GRC-enhanced binary tournament operator providing a comprehensive indicator to guide the search toward the Pareto front; 3) we demonstrate, on benchmark MOO and a NASDAQ case study, that the method delivers improved convergence and well-populated frontiers supporting actionable insights.

</details>


### [118] [Normalized Rewards for Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16240)
*Shawn Im,Federico Danieli,Skyler Seto,Barry-John Theobald,Katherine Metcalf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对直接对齐算法（DAAs）如DPO在微调LLM时对隐式奖励模型过度优化、导致偏好响应概率下降的问题，提出了添加正则化项来维持选择与拒绝响应的长度归一化概率的解决方案。研究表明，概率变化主要由少数异常token驱动，这解释了DAAs在降低选择响应概率的同时仍能提升生成质量的现象。将所提正则化应用于DPO和SimPO后，在AlpacaEval2上提升>20%，通用基准提升>9%，有效缓解了异常token的位移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DAAs（如DPO）在训练中会过度优化隐式奖励模型，导致偏好响应（chosen responses）的似然下降。这种似然下降可能引发不良行为，亟需缓解这一副作用。

Method: 提出在DAAs目标函数中添加正则化项，用于维持选择响应和拒绝响应的长度归一化概率。分别应用于基于参考的DPO和无参考的SimPO方法。通过分析token级别的似然变化分布，揭示了异常token是概率变化的主要来源。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，AlpacaEval2得分相对提升>20%，通用基准性能相对提升>9%。正则化有效缓解了偏好响应（尤其是异常token）的位移，在生成质量与通用能力之间实现了更好的权衡。

Conclusion: 添加正则化项能有效抑制DAAs的过度优化问题，在保持生成质量提升的同时显著改善通用基准性能，并通过控制异常token的似然变化来提升奖励建模能力。

Abstract: Direct Alignment Algorithms (DAAs) such as DPO have become a common way to post-train and align LLMs with human preferences. However, DAAs have been observed to over-optimize their implicit reward model and decrease the likelihood of preferred responses. This results in a decrease in the total likelihood assigned to responses seen in the preference dataset, potentially resulting in undesirable behavior. To counteract this undesired side-effect of DAAs, we examine the effect of using objectives that add a regularization term to maintain the total length-normalized probabilities of the chosen and rejected responses. To better understand over-optimization, we investigate how response likelihood changes are distributed over the tokens with and without regularization. We find that a significant portion of the likelihood changes are due to a small set of outlier tokens, which explains how DAAs improve generation quality despite decreasing the likelihoods of chosen responses. We apply the proposed regularization to reference-based (DPO) and reference-free (SimPO) methods and find (1) improved trade-offs between generation quality and general benchmark capability and (2) improvements in reward modeling across datasets. For example, on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, we see both a >20% relative increase in AlpacaEval2 scores and >9% relative performance gains on general benchmarks. Additionally, we find that the added regularization term effectively mitigates the amount of displacement within preferred responses overall, and for the outlier tokens specifically, by utilizing low-likelihood tokens.

</details>


### [119] [CIGPO: Contextual Information-Gain Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Evidence-Reading LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16244)
*Hao Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，在HotpotQA上使用GRPO训练多轮证据阅读智能体时，由于仅使用结果奖励，会出现零优势锁死（zero-advantage lock-in）问题：所有轨迹都受到最低格式惩罚，组内相对优势消失，策略梯度变为零，导致优化死锁。为此提出CIGPO方法，通过注入每步信息增益（IG）奖励来保持奖励方差，避免优势消失。CIGPO在3B规模下将F1从0.252提升至0.518，优于GRPO的最佳0.430，且避免了GRPO最终完全崩溃（F1=0.000）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于结果奖励的GRPO在多轮证据阅读任务中训练不稳定，中间步骤缺乏直接信用分配，容易导致优化崩溃。

Method: 提出CIGPO，通过冻结参考模型对正确答案的边际对数似然增量作为每步信号，结合IG奖励与F1奖励分别归一化及IG权重课程学习，维持奖励方差，防止零优势锁死。

Result: 在HotpotQA上，CIGPO达到标准F1 0.518（相比基线0.252提升105%），GRPO最佳仅0.430且最终崩溃至0.000。

Conclusion: 奖励方差崩溃是结果奖励GRPO的具体失败模式，每步IG奖励可以有效防止该问题。

Abstract: Training multi-turn evidence-reading agents with outcome-only reinforcement learning is unstable because intermediate turns receive little direct credit. In HotpotQA experiments with Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, GRPO initially improves (standard F1 0.430) but subsequently collapses to 100% format-violating outputs. Training-log diagnosis reveals a zero-advantage lock-in mechanism: all sampled trajectories receive the minimum format penalty (-2.0), group-relative advantages vanish, and the policy-gradient loss becomes zero--an optimization deadlock. We propose a variance-injection strategy: by assigning per-turn rewards to intermediate evidence-reading turns, we prevent the group reward distribution from collapsing to a single value--preserving the variation that GRPO's group-relative advantage requires. Contextual Information-Gain Policy Optimization (CIGPO) implements this strategy using the marginal increase in the frozen reference model's log-likelihood of the ground-truth answer as the per-turn signal. With separate normalization of IG and F1 rewards and an IG-weight curriculum, CIGPO reaches a standard F1 of 0.518 on HotpotQA at the 3B scale (from 0.252 base; +105%), compared with 0.430 for the best GRPO checkpoint and 0.000 for the final GRPO checkpoint. CIGPO maintains meaningful reward variance and avoids zero-advantage lock-in throughout training. These results identify reward-variance collapse as a concrete failure mode of outcome-only GRPO and show that turn-level IG rewards can prevent it in this HotpotQA setting.

</details>


### [120] [Self-Evolving Just-In-Time Memory for Proactive Embodied Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16247)
*Bingrui Sima,Lizhong Wang,Xiaoya Lu,Kun He,Xiao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Self-Evolving Just-In-Time Memory (JIT-Memory)框架，通过风险充分拓扑信念图、基于代理的事实记忆和经验记忆三个组件，以及自动化测试-验证-写入循环，将具身安全从阻碍进展的护栏转变为主动的风险缓解，在IS-Bench上显著提升了多个VLM骨干的安全成功率（如Qwen3-VL-8B提升30.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM具身代理在闭环交互中难以主动处理动态涌现的危险，而现有的安全方法要么运行时阻断不安全行为，要么导致过度谨慎，严重阻碍任务进展，无法主动解决潜在风险。

Method: 提出JIT-Memory框架，包含：(1) Risk-Sufficient Topological Belief Graph (RSG)用于在部分可观测性下持续跟踪安全相关状态；(2) Agency-Grounded Factual Memory用于精确预测危险；(3) Experience Memory注入程序性元技能以指导可执行的、不阻碍进展的缓解措施；以及自动化的Test-Verify-Write循环，使代理能在测试时从执行轨迹中持续优化元技能。

Result: 在IS-Bench上的实验表明，该框架在多个VLM骨干上显著提升了安全成功率，例如在Qwen3-VL-8B上提升了30.3%。

Conclusion: JIT-Memory框架成功打破了安全与进展之间的权衡，使代理能够在不阻碍任务进展的前提下主动缓解危险，为具身安全提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have empowered embodied agents to execute complex household tasks, they struggle to proactively handle dynamically emerging hazards during closed-loop interactions. Existing safety approaches often rely on runtime guardrails to block unsafe actions or induce excessive caution, which severely stalls task progress instead of actively resolving the underlying risks. To break this safety-progress trade-off, we introduce the Self-Evolving Just-In-Time Memory framework, which reframes embodied safety from progress-stalling guardrails to proactive hazard mitigation. The framework consists of a Risk-Sufficient Topological Belief Graph (RSG) for persistent safety-relevant state tracking under partial observability, an Agency-Grounded Factual Memory for precise hazard anticipation, and an Experience Memory that injects procedural Meta-Skills to guide executable, progress-preserving mitigation. Furthermore, we propose an automated Test-Verify-Write loop, allowing agents to continually refine their mitigation Meta-Skills from execution traces at test time. Experiments on IS-Bench demonstrate that our framework substantially boosts the Safe-Success rate across multiple VLM backbones (e.g., +30.3% on Qwen3-VL-8B), enabling agents to proactively mitigate hazards without stalling task progress. Code is available at https://github.com/DyMessi/JIT-Memory.

</details>


### [121] [From Outcomes to Actions: Leveraging Hindsight for Long-Horizon Language Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16257)
*Zishang Jiang,Tingyun Li,Jinyi Han,Xinyi Wang,Sihang Jiang,Yizhou Ying,Xiaojun Meng,Jiansheng Wei,Jiaqing Liang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对强化学习在长程交互任务中面临的高方差优化问题，本文提出了一种基于意图空间的 hindsight 策略优化方法（HPO），通过将当前策略分布与事后分布投影到意图空间并计算 Wasserstein 距离作为低方差学习信号，实现了稳定且高效的政策优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RL 方法在训练具有长程交互的智能体时面临挑战，主要瓶颈在于难以区分长程交互中不同动作的贡献，导致优化方差过高，影响训练的稳定性和效率。

Method: 提出 Hindsight Policy Optimization (HPO) 方法，将当前策略分布与事后分布投影到一个意图空间（intent space），通过计算二者之间的 Wasserstein 距离来提取低方差的学习信号，从而降低优化方差。

Result: 理论和实验均表明，在意图空间中对语义相似的状态和动作进行聚合能够产生有界方差的估计器，并稳定提升策略性能。

Conclusion: HPO 方法通过引入意图空间投影和 Wasserstein 距离，有效解决了长程交互 RL 中的高方差问题，为提升 LLM 在复杂长程任务上的训练稳定性提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a widely adopted technique for improving large language models (LLMs) on complex tasks. Despite this progress, existing RL methods still face challenges in training agents with longer-horizon interactions. One major bottleneck is distinguishing the contribution of different actions in long-horizon interaction, leading to high optimization variance. To address this, we introduce a novel policy gradient method, Hindsight Policy Optimization (HPO), that projects both the current policy distribution and the hindsight distribution into an intent space and extracts low-variance learning signals from the Wasserstein distance between them. We theoretically and empirically show that aggregating semantically similar states and actions in the intent space yields a bounded-variance estimator and improves policy performance stably. Our code is available online.

</details>


### [122] [A Predict-then-Correct Loop Based on Few-Shot Continuous Contextual Bandit for Demand Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16354)
*Zhiwei Lei,Benedict Jun Ma,Ilya Jackson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出预测-修正框架（PtC），通过少样本连续上下文强盗策略结合相似SKU增强和top-p掩码更新，在不重新训练基础模型的情况下实现零售需求在线自适应修正，在沃尔玛和饮料数据集上显著提升预测精度并降低库存成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售需求在早期需求周期中变化快于静态预测模型的重训练速度，且新观测标签稀疏，导致传统预测方法难以适应快速变化的需求模式。

Method: 提出PtC框架：第一阶段用机器学习生成初始预测，第二阶段采用少样本连续上下文强盗策略进行在线修正，核心组件包括相似SKU增强和top-p掩码更新机制。

Result: 在沃尔玛和饮料数据集上，PtC在稳定高量、稳定低量、间歇性等需求模式下均显著降低MAPE/MAE/RMSE；消融实验中平均RMSE较ML基线提升9.52%；在测试的交货期设置下库存成本低于base-stock、PPO和SAC策略。

Conclusion: 在线预测修正可有效桥接离线需求学习与实时零售决策，通过适应稀疏反馈信号提升预测性能，无需完全重新训练基础模型。

Abstract: Retail demand forecasting remains difficult when demand shifts faster than static forecasting models can be retrained, especially in early demand cycles where newly observed labels are sparse. To address this, this study aims to improve adaptive retail forecasting by proposing a predict-then-correct (PtC) framework that retains a first-stage machine learning (ML) forecast and applies a few-shot continuous contextual bandit correction policy with similar-SKUs augmentation and top-p masked updating. Across Walmart retail data and an exclusive beverage dataset, PtC delivers statistically significant reductions in MAPE, MAE, and RMSE across stable & high volume, stable & low volume, and erratic & intermittent demand patterns, improves average RMSE by 9.52% over the ML-only baseline in the ablation study, and yields lower inventory costs than base-stock, proximal policy optimization, and soft actor-critic policies under the tested lead-time settings. These findings show that online forecast correction can bridge offline demand learning and real-time retail decision-making by adapting to sparse feedback without fully retraining the base forecasting model.

</details>


### [123] [Feedback Attribution and Representation Geometry: Metrics for Comparing Individual and Shared Rewards in MARL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16524)
*Tasha Pais,Richard Higgins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体强化学习中团队平均奖励是否会在智能体的表示和行为中留下可测量的特征。提出EffRank/$n$（有效秩归一化）和$D_\text{act}$（智能体动作分布间的平均KL散度）作为低开销诊断指标，在SMACv2环境下测试。发现：几何结构主要由观测决定而非奖励分配方式；个体奖励下行为多样性更高；遮挡观测会显著降低探测信号。EffRank/$n$和$D_\text{act}$的额外开销小于5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究合作多智能体强化学习系统中使用团队平均奖励（即每个智能体获得相同团队结果，而不考虑个体贡献）这一常见做法，是否会在智能体学习的表征中留下可测量的几何或行为特征。

Method: 提出两种低开销诊断指标：EffRank/$n$（有效秩除以智能体数量，用于衡量表征几何结构）和$D_\text{act}$（智能体动作分布间的平均KL散度，用于衡量行为多样性）。在SMACv2的protoss_5_vs_5场景中，训练MAPPO智能体，进行观测类型（单位类型可见 vs. 掩码）×奖励分配方式（个体伤害贡献奖励 vs. 共享团队奖励）的对比实验。

Result: 几何结构（EffRank/$n$和探测准确率）主要受观测影响而非奖励方式：单位类型可见时，共享与个体奖励的EffRank/$n$分别为0.31±0.03和0.29±0.02，探测准确率分别为0.75±0.05和0.73±0.05（均远超随机1/3）。行为多样性（$D_\text{act}$）在个体奖励下更高（1.23±0.06 vs. 1.07±0.20）。遮挡单位类型后，探测信号从远高于随机降至0.49，降幅超一半。

Conclusion: 个体奖励下的智能体是称职且按角色可分的，但在SMACv2中观测解释了表征几何结构，奖励分配主要体现在行为层面。因此几何诊断必须控制观测到的角色信息，并测试未直接观测到的持久角色。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent RL systems routinely use team-averaged rewards, a feedback-attribution choice that gives each agent the team outcome regardless of its individual contribution. We ask whether this leaves a measurable signature, geometric or behavioral, on learned representations. We propose EffRank/$n$ (effective rank normalized by agent count) and $D_\text{act}$ (mean pairwise KL divergence between agents' action distributions) as low-overhead diagnostics for reward-attribution effects, then test them on competent MAPPO agents in SMACv2 \texttt{protoss\_5\_vs\_5}, where unit type is encoded in the observation. In an observation $\times$ reward-attribution comparison (unit type observed vs.\ masked; individual damage-contribution reward vs.\ shared team reward), geometry follows observation rather than reward. With unit type observed, shared and individual rewards have similar EffRank/$n$ ($0.31{\pm}0.03$ vs.\ $0.29{\pm}0.02$) and probe accuracy ($0.75{\pm}0.05$ vs.\ $0.73{\pm}0.05$, both $\gg 1/3$ chance), while $D_\text{act}$ leans higher under individual rewards ($1.23{\pm}0.06$ vs.\ $1.07{\pm}0.20$). Masking unit type cuts the above-chance probe signal by more than half, to $0.49$ in both reward arms. In short: individually rewarded agents are competent and separable by role, but on SMACv2 the observation explains the geometry and reward attribution shows up mainly in behavior. Thus geometric diagnostics must control for observed role information and test persistent roles that are not directly observed. EffRank/$n$ and $D_\text{act}$ add $<$5\% overhead.

</details>


### [124] [Building2Building: A Large Scale Benchmark for Generalizable Real-World Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16534)
*Vincent Taboga,Justin Veilleux,Doseok Jang,Anushree Rankawat,Pierre-Luc Bacon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: B2B是一个基于EnergyPlus的大规模HVAC控制环境套件，通过参数化建筑生成器创建多样化配置，并定义了面向目标适应、动力学适应、动作空间迁移和跨域迁移等RL开放挑战的基准任务，旨在系统研究连续控制中的泛化和迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL策略在环境变化（动力学、动作空间、观测空间、目标）时表现脆弱，而现有基准在多样性和复杂度上不足以支撑迁移学习、多任务学习和元学习的系统研究。

Method: 基于EnergyPlus建筑模拟器构建了与Gymnasium接口兼容的B2B套件，核心是参数化建筑生成器，可系统生成具有异构观测和动作空间的多样化建筑配置，并据此定义了多项面向泛化挑战的基准任务。

Result: 构建了一个大规模、多样化、物理真实的测试平台，配以标准化评估协议，可系统研究连续控制中的泛化与迁移问题。

Conclusion: B2B不仅推动了RL泛化研究，还通过实现大规模HVAC优化控制产生重要的社会影响，因为HVAC是建筑中能耗最高的系统之一。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has achieved strong results in control, yet learned policies remain brittle to changes in dynamics, action spaces, observation spaces, or goals, a critical limitation for real-world deployment. Existing benchmarks offer limited diversity and complexity, making it difficult to rigorously study transfer, multi-task learning, and meta-learning in RL. We introduce Building2Building (B2B), a large-scale suite of realistic Heating, Ventilation, and Air Conditioning (HVAC) control environments built on EnergyPlus, a state-of-the-art building simulator. B2B is fully compatible with the Gymnasium interface and features a parametric building generator, enabling the systematic generation of diverse building configurations with heterogeneous observation and action spaces. Based on this suite, we define benchmark tasks targeting key open challenges in RL, including goal adaptation, dynamics adaptation, action-space shifts, and cross-domain transfer. By providing a large-scale, diverse, and physically grounded testbed with standardized evaluation protocols, B2B enables systematic investigation of generalization and transfer in continuous control. Beyond advancing research on generalization in RL, this new benchmark also carries significant societal implications by enabling improved HVAC control at scale, one of the most energy-intensive systems in buildings.

</details>


### [125] [Learning from World Feedback: Why Model Uncertainty Fails as a Risk Signal in Model-Based RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16591)
*Zhaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RLxF框架，主张学习信号应来自世界反馈而非模型内部代理。在安全模型预测控制中，发现基于动态不确定性的安全代理与真实安全性呈反相关（碰撞率从26%升至34%），而使用三种世界反馈信号（LiDAR最小距离、碰撞时间、结果监督反馈模型）可将碰撞率降至1-14%。机制上，模型不确定性与任务风险在支持空间上正交（r<0.15），并提炼出三条RLxF原则，适用于模型控制和大模型对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的控制方法（如MBRL）使用模型内部的不确定性作为安全代理，但这些代理信号与真实世界反馈之间存在偏差，可能导致安全性能下降。作者旨在论证学习信号应直接来源于世界反馈而非模型内部代理。

Method: 在四种不同精度的世界模型架构（覆盖2倍MSE范围）上，使用TOST统计检验比较MPC控制性能；对比基于动态不确定性的安全代理与世界反馈信号（LiDAR最小距离、碰撞时间、结果监督反馈模型g_psi）的效果；分析模型不确定性与任务风险在支持空间上的相关性。

Result: 模型不确定性惩罚使碰撞率从26%升至34%，而世界反馈信号将碰撞率降至1-14%；模型不确定性与任务风险的相关性r<0.15；基于结果监督的反馈模型g_psi在结构上类比于RLHF中的奖励模型。

Conclusion: 提出了三条RLxF原则：将风险基于世界结果、部署前验证代理、无法直接获取世界信号时使用结果训练的替代反馈模型。这些原则同样适用于基于验证器或RLHF的大语言模型对齐方法。

Abstract: The RLxF programme argues that learning signals should come from world feedback rather than from internal model proxies. We instantiate this position in safe model-based control and distil it into three concrete design principles. Empirically, across four world-model architectures spanning a 2x MSE range, MPC planning is statistically equivalent (TOST, n=200), and dynamics-based uncertainty penalties increase collision rates from 26% to 34%: the standard MBRL safety proxy is anti-correlated with safety in this regime. Replacing the model-internal proxy with three world-feedback signals (a sensor-derived margin via minimum lidar, a temporal signal via time-to-collision, and an outcome-supervised feedback model g_psi trained on prior collision labels, structurally analogous to outcome-trained reward models in RLHF) reduces collisions to 1-14% without retraining the world model or the planner. The mechanism is structural: model uncertainty has support over state-prediction space, whereas task risk has support over constraint boundaries, with empirical correlation r < 0.15. From this we extract three RLxF principles (ground risk in world outcomes, validate proxies before deployment, and substitute outcome-trained feedback models when direct world signals are unavailable) and argue they apply equally to model-based control and to verifier-based or RLHF approaches in LLM alignment.

</details>


### [126] [Rethinking the Suitability of Reinforcement Learning Algorithms Under Practical Transfer Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17326)
*Hany Hamed,Abhishek Naik,Colin Bellinger,A. Rupam Mahmood*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出迁移式强化学习中，样本效率不足以评估算法优劣，实际训练时间（墙钟时间）和领域随机化的鲁棒性同样关键。实验发现，样本效率较低的PPO算法在并行训练下能更快获得可用策略；领域随机化对PPO、SAC、TD-MPC2三种不同范式算法的影响模式相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迁移式强化学习评估过度关注样本效率，忽略了实际训练时间和动力学失配下的鲁棒性。作者希望通过这两个维度揭示算法评估的不足，为实践者提供更具实用性的指导。

Method: 对三种代表性RL范式（PPO: on-policy, SAC: off-policy, TD-MPC2: model-based）进行受控对比实验，在同一种迁移协议下，分别从墙钟时间效率和领域随机化覆盖度两个维度进行系统评估。

Result: 1) PPO尽管样本效率低，但由于可大规模并行化，实际墙钟时间更快训练出可用策略；2) 领域随机化对三种不同范式算法的影响趋势相似，均能帮助学习鲁棒策略。

Conclusion: 迁移式RL算法评估不能仅看样本效率，还需考虑实际训练时间等实践因素；领域随机化对主流RL范式具有普遍适用性。

Abstract: Transfer-oriented reinforcement learning requires evaluating algorithms along dimensions that go beyond standard sample efficiency. We focus on two dimensions: practical efficiency, which asks whether conclusions about algorithm suitability change under wall-clock rather than interaction-based budgets, and robustness under dynamics mismatch, which asks how different learning paradigms respond to variability in the training distribution induced by domain randomization. We provide two insights to reinforcement-learning practitioners. First, comparing the sample efficiency of different algorithms is often an insufficient criterion in transfer-oriented settings. The wall-clock time required to train a decent policy is an important consideration for practitioners, and we find that the sample-inefficient PPO algorithm can produce a performant policy faster than relatively more sample-efficient algorithms such as SAC and TD-MPC2, validating the common understanding of massively parallel training paradigms. Second, domain randomization can help different kinds of algorithms learn robust policies. In particular, although PPO, SAC, and TD-MPC2 represent different RL paradigms - on-policy, off-policy, and model-based learning and planning, respectively - we find that domain randomization affects all three algorithms in a similar way. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first controlled comparison of the effect of domain-randomization coverage on PPO, SAC, and TD-MPC2 under the same transfer protocol. Taken together, these two insights highlight the importance of evaluating RL algorithms not only by sample efficiency, but also by practical considerations such as training time and the algorithms' ability to produce usable policies.

</details>


### [127] [Generalize and Guide: Decomposing Rewards for Few-Shot Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17760)
*Ziyi Liu,Grace Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MPG方法，通过可泛化的判别器与邻近函数两个互补组件，解决少样本多任务逆向强化学习（FM-IRL）问题，在导航与操作任务中平均成功率81.2%，比最强基线高出24.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实任务常存在大量自然变化（如不同形状的杯子），难以收集覆盖所有场景的目标任务演示。虽然目标任务演示有限，但容易获得异构但相关的多任务演示数据集。因此需要解决少样本IRL问题，从少量目标任务演示和充足的相关任务演示中快速学习新任务。

Method: 提出多任务判别器邻近引导IRL（MPG），包含两个互补的奖励组件：(1) 可泛化判别器，跨相关任务迁移共享结构，在新任务中识别专家行为；(2) 邻近函数，衡量状态偏离专家行为的程度，在探索过程中提供修正引导。

Result: 在多个具有显著变化的导航与操作任务（物体配置、桌面布局、机器人初始姿态）上，平均成功率81.2%，比最强单任务基线平均高出24.7个百分点。

Conclusion: MPG通过结合可迁移的判别器与邻近修正函数，有效解决了少样本多任务IRL问题，显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a powerful framework for learning from demonstrations. However, real-world tasks often exhibit substantial natural variations (e.g., picking up mugs with varying shapes), making it impractical to collect demonstrations that fully specify a new task under every possible scenario. In practice, while demonstrations for the target task are limited, it is often easier to obtain datasets of heterogeneous but related behaviors. This motivates the problem of few-shot IRL with multi-task demonstrations (FM-IRL), where an agent must learn a new task with substantial variations from only a limited number of target-task demonstrations, together with sufficient demonstrations of related tasks and online agent experience. To do so, we must both recover the expert distribution of the new task and provide guidance when the agent deviates from it. We introduce Multitask discriminator Proximity-Guided IRL (MPG), which learns two complementary reward components: (1) a generalizable discriminator that transfers shared structure across related tasks to identify expert behavior in a new task, and (2) a proximity function that measures how far a state deviates from expert behavior and provides corrective guidance during exploration. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method on multiple challenging navigation and manipulation tasks under significant variations (e.g., object configurations, table layouts, and initial robot poses), achieving an average success rate of 81.2%, outperforming the strongest per-task baseline by an average of 24.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [128] [Principled Direction-Free Intrinsic Motivation through Model-Free Epistemic Free-Energy Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16858)
*Alireza Furutanpey,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于期望自由能（Expected Free Energy）的无偏好内在奖励，通过参数信息增益（epistemic value）驱动探索、罚项捕捉aleatoric方差、短视门控保护信息性后继状态，在混合不确定性环境中实现平稳的强化学习，无需显式预测模型或切换策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督强化学习中的内在动机方法存在不足：惊奇最小化局限于"不稳定"环境；基于预测误差的好奇心奖励包含不可约噪声；混合切换策略人为引入非平稳性。需要一种能自适应处理混合不确定性来源、无需预先承诺特定惊奇方向的单内在奖励信号。

Method: 1) 将无偏好的期望自由能目标转化为奖励最大化形式，核心信号为参数信息增益（参数信息增益 = 下一状态期望惊奇 - 不可约惊奇部分）；2) 在未解析动力学区域，epistemic项驱动探索；动力学解析后epistemic项消失，aleatoric惩罚偏向低方差转移；3) 使用伪计数提供epistemic价值，探针式惩罚捕捉aleatoric方差，短视门控保护信息性后继；4) 窗口冻结所有奖励定义对象，实现平稳Bellman算子、显式学习目标边界及非参数估计量的条件一致收敛性结果。

Result: 在混合、平滑性、带宽和容量假设下，窗口冻结机制保证了平稳Bellman算子、学习目标的显式边界，以及非参数估计量的条件一致收敛性。该方法在主动推理框架下，无偏好保留新颖性，完全可观测下标准似然模糊性消失，添加非标准转移熵惩罚，在已解析状态空间区域涌现惊奇最小化行为。

Conclusion: 参数信息增益是混合不确定性环境中无监督强化学习的恰当内在信号，它能自适应地驱动探索已解析动力学区域的低方差转移，同时避免预承诺单一惊奇方向或引入非平稳性。该工作为主动推理与强化学习之间提供了理论桥梁。

Abstract: Across environments with mixed sources of uncertainty, unsupervised reinforcement learning requires intrinsic motivation that does not precommit to a particular direction of surprise. Surprise minimization is scoped by design to ``unstable'' environments. Prediction-error curiosity rewards total expected surprise, including irreducible noise. Bandit or mixture switching between surprise-minimizing and surprise-maximizing rewards reintroduces non-stationarity by construction. We propose a single intrinsic reward, stationary within each window, derived from the novelty contribution of a preference-free Expected Free Energy objective, expressed in reward-maximization form. Our claim is that parameter information gain, the expected surprise of the next state minus its irreducible part, is the appropriate intrinsic signal in both high-entropy and low-entropy components of the state space. Maximizing it seeks exactly the surprise the model can explain away. In regions of unresolved dynamics, this epistemic term drives exploration. As dynamics become resolved, the epistemic term vanishes, while an aleatoric penalty favors lower-variance transitions, all without fitting an explicit next-state predictor. A pseudocount supplies epistemic value, a probe-based penalty captures aleatoric variance, and a short-horizon gate protects informative successors. A window-based freeze of all reward-defining objects yields a stationary Bellman operator, explicit bounds on learning targets, and a conditional uniform-concentration result for the nonparametric estimators under mixing, smoothness, bandwidth, and capacity assumptions. In active-inference terms, the agent is preference-free where novelty is retained, standard likelihood ambiguity vanishes under full observability, a nonstandard transition-entropy penalty is added, and surprise minimization emerges in resolved regions of the state space.

</details>


### [129] [Enhancing Personalized Bladder Cancer Treatment Through Reinforcement Learning: A Recurrent Patient State Transition Decision Support Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16916)
*Divyansh Chawla,Anshu Garg,Isshaan Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种递归患者状态转换模拟框架，结合马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）和深度Q网络（DQN）强化学习，用于膀胱癌治疗计划的动态优化，在模拟环境中实现了63,918.87的累积奖励和6.62%的策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的临床决策支持系统依赖静态治疗指南或单步预测模型，无法捕捉疾病随时间进展的动态变化，特别是在复发性膀胱癌中治疗效果随临床治疗序列不断变化，需要个性化和自适应的决策支持。

Method: 框架包含两个核心模块：1) 预测性状态转换模块，估算治疗后肿瘤特征的变化；2) 强化学习模块（MDP + DQN），通过与模拟患者轨迹交互，序贯优化治疗决策。框架能够生成可解释的治疗轨迹和详细的模拟日志。

Result: 与现有基于强化学习的治疗规划方法相比，该框架实现了63,918.87的累积奖励、每回合0.0056的平均训练损失和6.62%的策略改进评分，证明了有效的序贯学习和鲁棒的治疗优化能力。

Conclusion: 递归患者状态转换模拟结合强化学习可作为灵活的决策支持框架，用于个性化膀胱癌治疗规划和AI辅助精准肿瘤学。

Abstract: Bladder cancer treatment requires personalized and adaptive decision-making, particularly for recurrent disease, where treatment effectiveness changes across successive clinical episodes. Conventional clinical decision support systems typically rely on static treatment guidelines or single-step predictive models, limiting their ability to capture disease progression over time. This paper presents a recurrent patient state-transition simulation framework for bladder cancer treatment planning that integrates predictive state-transition modeling with a Markov Decision Process (MDP) and a Deep Q-Network (DQN) reinforcement learning environment. The predictive module estimates changes in tumor characteristics following treatment, while the reinforcement learning agent sequentially optimizes treatment decisions by interacting with simulated patient trajectories. This framework enables dynamic, patient-specific treatment planning by continuously adapting recommendations to evolving clinical states. It also generates interpretable treatment trajectories and detailed simulation logs to improve transparency and support clinical decision-making. The proposed framework was evaluated against existing reinforcement learning-based treatment planning approaches. It achieved a cumulative reward of 63,918.87, an average training loss per episode of 0.0056, and a policy improvement score of 6.62%, demonstrating effective sequential learning and robust treatment optimization in a simulated recurrent treatment environment. These findings highlight the potential of recurrent patient state-transition simulation with reinforcement learning as a flexible decision-support framework for personalized bladder cancer treatment planning and AI-assisted precision oncology.

</details>


### [130] [Counterfactual Shapley Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16999)
*Mingxuan Li,Kaizhan-Lee,Elias Bareinboim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反事实Shapley值的信用分配框架（φ-value），通过因果理论重新分配环境奖励，解决了强化学习中技能（policy）与运气（stochasticity）的归因问题，并提出φ-PPO算法结合优先轨迹回放（PTR），在稀疏因果、高随机性和延迟奖励场景下显著提升样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习信用分配方法（如时间连续性或事后奖励重加权）无法正确区分智能体的策略（技能）与环境随机性（运气）对结果的因果贡献，导致归因偏差。需要一种基于因果理论的原理性框架来隔离真实的因果驱动因素。

Method: 提出Counterfactual Shapley Credit Assignment框架，利用反事实Shapley值（φ-value）进行因果归因；推导了一致性估计量以高效计算φ-value；将φ-value与PPO结合得到φ-PPO算法，并引入优先轨迹回放（PTR）机制。

Result: 在稀疏因果、高随机性和延迟奖励的挑战性环境中，φ-value能够精确对齐任务奖励的真实因果原因，φ-PPO在样本效率上优于现有最先进方法，在先前方法无法收敛的场景下仍能有效学习。

Conclusion: 反事实Shapley信用分配提供了从因果角度解决CAP的数学原理性框架，在保持最优策略的同时显著提升了三个关键维度上的信用分配质量，且计算高效，适用于实际强化学习应用。

Abstract: The Credit Assignment Problem (CAP) is fundamental to developing efficient and explainable Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents. Existing frameworks, whether relying on temporal contiguity or hindsight-conditioned reward reweighting, frequently fail to attribute properly between an agent's policy (skill) and environmental stochasticity (luck). A principled approach to CAP must isolate the true causal drivers of observed outcomes from spurious correlations and environmental randomness. We introduce Counterfactual Shapley Credit Assignment, a novel framework grounded in causal theory that attributes credit and blame via the Counterfactual Shapley Value ($φ$-value). By redistributing environmental rewards, $φ$-values enhance temporal credit assignment across three critical dimensions: sparse causality, high stochasticity, and delayed rewards, all while preserving the optimal policy. We derive a consistent estimator that computes $φ$-values efficiently, enabling a new class of policy gradient methods, $φ$-PPO, combined with Prioritized Trajectory Replay (PTR). Empirical results demonstrate that $φ$-values align precisely to the ground truth causes of task rewards with superior sample efficiency in challenging environments where prior state-of-the-art methods fail to converge.

</details>


### [131] [Scalable Causal Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17003)
*Eylam Tagor,Mingxuan Li,Elias Bareinboim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种离线策略因果模仿学习算法——Causal SQIL和Causal IQ-Learn，通过将因果调整框架与逆强化学习目标结合，并利用滑动窗口近似序贯π-后门准则，有效解决了长时域、高维连续控制任务中的因果混淆问题，显著优于现有因果模仿学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果模仿学习方法（Causal BC和Causal GAIL）仅适用于短时域、低维环境，在长时域、高维连续控制任务中表现不佳：Causal BC存在复合误差累积，Causal GAIL训练不稳定且样本效率低，序贯π-后门调整在实际中不可行。因此需要设计适用于复杂连续控制任务的因果模仿学习算法。

Method: 1) 将因果调整框架与离线策略逆强化学习目标（SQIL和IQ-Learn）相结合；2) 利用连续控制环境的因果结构，将全时域序贯π-后门调整近似为固定大小的滑动窗口，生成因果调整后的状态表示；3) 在该表示上训练离线策略模仿学习算法。

Result: 在一组存在混淆变量的环境中评估，Causal SQIL和Causal IQ-Learn在长时域任务上显著优于现有CIL算法，有时甚至超越专家性能；而所有非因果的模仿学习方法均无法学习到有意义的行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的两种离线策略因果模仿学习算法成功将因果调整方法扩展到长时域、高维连续控制任务，为解决现实场景中观察不匹配和未观测混淆变量带来的模仿学习困难提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Imitation learning enables learning a policy in an unknown environment with a latent reward signal using expert demonstrations, but it struggles when the imitator's and expert's observations are mismatched and unobserved confounders are present in expert demonstrations. By identifying appropriate adjustment sets via the sequential $π$-backdoor criterion, causal imitation learning (CIL) provides a framework for approximating the expert's policy from confounded data. However, existing CIL methods, Causal Behavioral Cloning (Causal BC) and Causal Generative Adversarial Imitation Learning (Causal GAIL), are designed for short-horizon, low-dimensional settings. When applied to continuous control tasks with long horizons and high-dimensional state-action spaces, these methods exhibit poor performance: Causal BC suffers from compounding errors, Causal GAIL is unstable and sample-inefficient, and sequential $π$-backdoor adjustment becomes impractical. We introduce Causal Soft Q Imitation Learning (SQIL) and Causal Inverse soft-Q Learning (IQ-Learn), two off-policy causal imitation learning algorithms that combine the causal adjustment framework with state-of-the-art inverse reinforcement learning objectives. Both algorithms operate on causally-adjusted state representations produced by an efficient approximation of the sequential $π$-backdoor criterion, exploiting the causal structure of continuous control environments to reduce the full-horizon adjustment to a fixed-size sliding window. We evaluate all methods in a suite of confounded environments and find that Causal SQIL and Causal IQ-Learn substantially outperform prior CIL algorithms on long-horizon tasks, sometimes surpassing the expert, whereas all causally unaware imitation methods fail to learn meaningful behavior.

</details>


### [132] [Distilled Reinforcement Learning for LLM Post-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17247)
*Chen Wang,Zhaochun Li,Jionghao Bai,Yining Zhang,Hexuan Deng,Ge Lan,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Distilled RL（蒸馏强化学习），将教师监督整合到RL目标中，通过反向重要性采样、负样本重置和序列级几何归一化，在避免无条件模仿的同时选择性传递新知识，在族内和跨族蒸馏中均优于标准RL和OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后训练方法存在两类局限：RL依赖粗粒度的结果监督，导致信用分配困难且知识获取能力有限；OPD通过KL散度无条件匹配教师logits，陷入教师相似则无新知识、教师差异大则指导无效的困境，严重限制了跨族蒸馏的有效性。

Method: Distilled RL将教师监督整合进RL目标函数，包含三个关键组件：1）反向重要性采样与裁剪（Reverse Importance Sampling with Clipping），用于选择性传递新知识；2）负样本重置（Negative Sample Reset），处理低质量样本；3）序列级几何归一化（Sequence-level Geometric Normalization），稳定训练过程。该方法在避免无条件模仿教师的同时提供细粒度指导。

Result: 通过简洁可解释的案例研究发现，Distilled RL能有效将教师模型中原本不可用的知识迁移到学生模型。在族内和跨族蒸馏场景的大规模实验中，Distilled RL在pass@1和pass@k指标上均显著优于标准RL和OPD。

Conclusion: Distilled RL成功融合了RL和OPD的优势，通过细粒度的教师指导克服了信用分配问题，同时避免了无条件模仿带来的知识迁移困境，为LLM后训练提供了一种更有效的范式，尤其是在跨族蒸馏场景中展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) post-training is essential for improving reasoning, adaptation, and alignment. Existing methods mainly follow two paradigms: reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD). However, RL relies on coarse-grained outcome supervision, resulting in difficult credit assignment and limited capability to acquire new knowledge. OPD, meanwhile, unconditionally matches teacher logits through KL divergence, which creates a dilemma: similar teachers provide little new knowledge, while substantially different teachers often yield ineffective guidance, largely restricting OPD to within-family distillation. We propose Distilled Reinforcement Learning (Distilled RL), which integrates teacher supervision into the RL objective to provide fine-grained guidance, selectively transfer new knowledge and avoid unconditional imitation. Distilled RL contains three components: reverse importance sampling with clipping, negative sample reset, and sequence-level geometric normalization. Through a concise and interpretable case study, we demonstrate that Distilled RL can effectively transfer previously unavailable knowledge from a teacher model to a student model. Extensive experiments across both within-family and cross-family distillation settings show that Distilled RL substantially outperforms standard RL and OPD in terms of both pass@1 and pass@k. Our code is available at https://github.com/597358816/Distilled-RL.

</details>


### [133] [AIGB-R1: Self-Evolving Generative Auto-Bidding via Hierarchical Planner-Executor Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17281)
*Yuejia Dou,Hesong Wang,Xinyu Zhang,Tianyu Wang,Zhilin Zhang,Chuan Yu,Jian Xu,Bo Zheng,Qi Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AIGB-R1框架，通过分层自演化架构结合大语言模型的推理能力，解决AI生成竞价中的离线数据模式覆盖有限和任务状态理解不足问题，在大规模公开数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成竞价(AIGB)范式依赖离线数据生成建模，面临模式覆盖有限和任务状态理解不足的局限，难以有效探索最优策略。大语言模型虽具备先验知识和推理能力，但直接应用于自动竞价存在数值精度有限、幻觉和推理延迟等挑战。

Method: 提出AIGB-R1分层自演化竞价框架，包含高层规划器(Planner)进行宏观策略规划和低层执行器(Executor)进行细粒度决策；设计经验驱动的自演化循环实现自主策略探索与优化；采用两阶段流程（离线预训练+后训练对齐）并构建交互式竞价仿真环境；提出解耦组相对策略优化(D-GRPO)实现端到端优化。

Result: 在大规模公开数据集上的实验结果表明AIGB-R1框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过LLM推理能力与分层自演化框架的结合，AIGB-R1有效克服了传统AIGB方法的局限，为在线广告自动竞价提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Auto-bidding plays an essential role in online advertising, automatically adjusting bids for advertisers to optimize their commercial goals. The emerging AI-Generated Bidding (AIGB) paradigm widely adopts generative modeling to optimize bidding strategies, yet suffers from the limited mode coverage of offline datasets and inadequate task-state understanding, hindering effective exploration of optimal strategies. Large Language Models (LLMs), with prior world knowledge and reasoning capabilities, offer a promising approach to overcome these limitations. However, directly applying LLMs to auto-bidding tasks faces inherent challenges in limited numerical precision, hallucinations, and inference latency. To address these limitations, we propose AIGB-R1, a hierarchical self-evolving auto-bidding framework aiming to enhance AI-Generated Bidding via LLMs' Reasoning capabilities, comprising a high-level Planner module for macro-level strategy planning and a low-level Executor module for fine-grained decision-making. Building upon this, we design an experience-driven self-evolving loop, enabling autonomous strategy exploration and optimization from accumulated experience. We adopt a two-stage pipeline of offline pre-training and post-training alignment, and build an interactive bidding simulation environment for strategy rollout. Furthermore, we propose Decoupled Group Relative Policy Optimization (D-GRPO) to achieve end-to-end optimization via advantage decoupling. Experimental results on a large-scale public dataset demonstrate the effectiveness of AIGB-R1.

</details>


### [134] [WAR: Workload-Aware Rollouts for Synchronous Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17299)
*Ryan Xu,Atlas Zhao,David Bao,Frank Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WAR是一个面向同步智能体强化学习的工作负载感知 rollout 系统，通过联合优化解码（低负载时使用 SuffixDecoding 无模型推测解码）和调度（高负载时使用缓存感知全局调度），在不同负载下分别提升长上下文 rollout 吞吐量 1.4x 和 1.6x，有效消除 rollout 瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程 rollout 生成已成为智能体强化学习（特别是同步训练）的主要系统瓶颈：轨迹迅速增长到数万 token，导致训练受限于 rollout 阶段。

Method: WAR提出两种负载感知的优化策略：(1) 低负载下使用 SuffixDecoding——复用已完成轨迹的后缀模式作为推测草案，无需额外草稿模型，避免 GPU 竞争；(2) 高负载下转向缓存感知调度——全局调度器根据缓存局部性、轨迹进度和服务器负载进行请求分配，减少 KV-cache 重计算和负载不均衡。

Result: 低负载下长上下文智能体 rollout 吞吐量提升 1.4x，高负载下提升 1.6x。

Conclusion: WAR在不改变 RL 算法的前提下，通过解码级与系统级联合优化，有效消除了同步智能体 RL 中的 rollout 瓶颈，为可扩展的长上下文智能体训练提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Long-horizon rollout generation has become the dominant systems bottleneck in agentic reinforcement learning (RL). As agents interact with environments over many turns, trajectories rapidly grow to tens of thousands of tokens, making synchronous RL training increasingly constrained by rollout. We propose WAR, a workload-aware rollout system that substantially accelerates synchronous agentic RL by jointly optimizing decoding and scheduling. WAR is built on a key observation: the optimal rollout optimization strategy depends on runtime load: (1) Under low load, WAR enables model-free speculative decoding with SuffixDecoding, which reuses suffix patterns from previously completed trajectories as speculative drafts for future rollouts. Unlike model-based drafters, SuffixDecoding introduces no additional draft model and avoids GPU contention with rollout generation. (2) Under high load, where saturated batched decoding leaves limited room for speculative speedup, WAR shifts the optimization focus to cache-aware scheduling. A global scheduler places requests across rollout replicas based on cache locality, trajectory progress and server load, reducing redundant KV-cache recomputation and mitigating load imbalance. By combining decoding-level suffix reuse with system-level rollout scheduling, WAR delivers robust throughput improvements across workload regimes without changing the underlying RL algorithm. WAR improves long-context agentic rollout throughput by 1.4x under low load and up to 1.6x under high load. These results show that WAR removes a major rollout bottleneck in synchronous agentic RL and provides a practical path toward scalable long-context agent training.

</details>


### [135] [Rationalizing Boltzmann Rationality: An Axiomatic Characterization of Entropy-Regularized Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17316)
*Silviu Pitis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过区分环境随机性（机会）和策略随机性（选择），证明在策略和价值函数上施加不相关替代独立性（IIA）和单调性公理，可唯一推导出Boltzmann策略、熵正则化表示和soft Bellman方程，从而调和softmax策略与MDP奖励结构独立性公理之间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL文献对softmax策略的论证缺乏第一性原理推导，其熵正则化项与MDP奖励结构所依赖的独立性公理存在基本张力，亟需从公理基础上统一解释soft与hard Bellman方程的选择问题。

Method: 将von Neumann-Morgenstern独立性公理限制在环境彩票（基础前景）上，区分"机会"和"选择"两种随机性，在策略和价值函数上施加IIA和单调性公理，推导出Boltzmann策略及熵正则化表示的唯一定理。

Result: 唯一推导出Boltzmann策略、熵正则化表示和soft Bellman方程；证明soft与hard Bellman方程的选择等价于智能体是否重视自身选择能力；展示在广义折扣下的回报单调性和收敛性。

Conclusion: softmax策略可从第一性原理公理推导得出，IIA在智能体设计中是否适用取决于设计目标；该框架统一了经济学和信息论中导出相同结构的独立研究路线，为RL中的随机策略设计提供了规范性评估基础。

Abstract: The softmax policy $π(a \mid s) \propto \exp(βQ(s,a))$ is the default model of stochastic choice in reinforcement learning (RL). Various justifications based on robustness, exploration, and optimization have been offered in the RL literature, but none uniquely derives the softmax form from first principles. This leaves a basic tension unresolved: the entropy bonus in the soft Bellman equation violates the Independence axiom that underwrites the Markov decision process (MDP) reward structure. We dissolve this tension by distinguishing two kinds of randomness: chance and choice. By restricting von Neumann-Morgenstern (VNM) Independence to environmental lotteries over base prospects, we show that imposing independence of irrelevant alternatives (IIA) and monotonicity on the policy and value functions at choice nodes uniquely determines the Boltzmann policy, the entropy-regularized representation, and the soft Bellman equation. The choice between the soft and hard Bellman equations thus reduces to a design decision: whether the agent values its own ability to choose. We develop RL-specific consequences, including return monotonicity and convergence under generalized discounting, and synthesize the independent lines from economics and information theory that arrive at the same structure, offering a normative assessment of when IIA is appropriate for agent design.

</details>


### [136] [TAPAS: Throughput-adaptive Perception for Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17317)
*Aman Vyas,Vasista Kodumagulla,Zain Taufique,Pasi Liljeberg,Anil Kanduri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TAPAS是一种面向移动/边缘平台的吞吐量自适应感知策略，通过强化学习和GRU智能体实现运行时动态资源分配，在不同场景复杂度下自适应调整FPS目标与模型-集群映射，在KITTI和nuScenes数据集上分别实现了93-100%和97%的吞吐量达标率，同时节省76%和64%的能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有感知策略采用固定FPS和静态模型-集群映射，无法适应运行时场景复杂度的动态变化，导致吞吐量过剩或不足以及不必要的能耗浪费。

Method: 提出TAPAS（吞吐量自适应感知策略），采用强化学习框架，结合RRM（奖励推理模型）和GRU（门控循环单元）智能体，在异构移动/边缘平台上实现感知任务的运行时智能编排。

Result: 在KITTI数据集测试序列上，吞吐量达标率93-100%，能耗降低76%；在未见过的nuScenes数据集上，吞吐量达标率97%，能耗较SOTA降低64%。

Conclusion: TAPAS通过场景复杂度感知与动态模型-集群映射的紧耦合设计，有效解决了移动/边缘平台感知模块的吞吐量与能耗权衡问题，并在未见场景中展现出良好的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Autonomous systems rely on a perception module to navigate through dynamic environments. In real-world scenarios, the perception module's throughput requirements vary at runtime due to changes in scene complexity. However, existing perception strategies assume a fixed FPS and static model-to-cluster mapping, resulting in either over/under provision of throughput requirements or unnecessary energy consumption across diverse scenes. Addressing this challenge requires tightly coupled \textit{scene complexity awareness} to estimate an appropriate FPS target and \textit{dynamic model-to-cluster mapping} to deliver the required throughput at minimum energy. We propose a throughput-adaptive perception strategy for mobile/edge platforms, enabling intelligent runtime resource allocation based on varying FPS targets. We use Reinforcement Learning (RL) with RRM (Reward Reasoning Model) and a GRU (Gated Recurrent Unit) agent to orchestrate perception tasks across heterogeneous mobile/edge platforms. We evaluate TAPAS on Jetson Orin NX across KITTI and unseen nuScenes. On the \textit{KITTI} dataset's test sequences, TAPAS achieves 93-100% throughput met rate while saving energy by 76%. On the unseen \textit{nuScenes} dataset, TAPAS maintains 97% throughput met rate with 64% lower energy compared to \textit{SOTA} approaches, proving its robustness.

</details>


### [137] [CORAL: Learning Amyloid Fibril Ligand Docking with Cooperative Binding Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17412)
*Yasheng Sun,Bohan Li,Youqi Tao,Jürgen Schmidhuber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CORAL框架，利用强化学习训练生成式对接模型，专门针对淀粉样纤维的交叉β沟槽几何结构进行配体对接，通过合作性配体-配体堆叠能量和蛋白质-配体对接亲和力作为奖励信号，解决了淀粉样纤维-配体复合物结构稀缺和结合模式特殊的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淀粉样纤维与配体的共晶结构极为稀缺（即使cryo-EM进步也只解析了少量结构），导致有监督对接模型训练不可行；同时，配体在淀粉样纤维中的结合模式与球状蛋白完全不同——配体嵌入纵向交叉β沟槽并沿纤维轴合作性堆叠，现有对接模型无法捕捉这种几何结构。

Method: 采用强化学习框架，训练生成式对接模型产生适配交叉β沟槽几何的配体姿势分布；奖励函数合作性结合了配体-配体堆叠能量（合作性）和蛋白质-配体对接亲和力，以捕捉淀粉样纤维独特的结合几何；并构建了由模型生成姿势经领域专家验证的评估集。

Result: 在实验解析结构和评估集上，CORAL相比现有对接基线方法在姿势质量和结合亲和力相关性上均有提升。

Conclusion: CORAL能够有效处理淀粉样纤维这类特殊靶标的配体对接问题，为神经退行性疾病相关淀粉样蛋白的诊断、成像探针和治疗药物设计提供了可行工具。

Abstract: A hallmark of neurodegenerative diseases such as Alzheimer's and Parkinson's is the aberrant aggregation of proteins into amyloid fibrils, and small molecules that selectively bind to these fibrils hold promise as diagnostics, imaging probes, and therapeutics. Predicting how such ligands bind to fibril targets, however, presents two fundamental challenges. First, resolved co-crystal structures of amyloid-ligand complexes are exceptionally scarce; even with recent advances in cryo-EM only a handful have been structurally characterized, making supervised training of docking models impractical for this target class. Second, amyloid fibrils present a binding mode fundamentally different from globular proteins: ligands intercalate into longitudinal cross-$β$ grooves and stack cooperatively along the fibril axis, a geometry that existing docking models are not designed to capture. To address these challenges, we present CORAL (COopeRative Amyloid Ligand docking), a reinforcement learning framework that trains a generative docking model to produce ligand pose distributions tailored to the cross-$β$ groove geometry. Our reward explicitly incorporates cooperative ligand-ligand stacking energy alongside protein-ligand docking affinity, directly capturing the distinctive binding geometry of amyloid fibrils. We further introduce a curated evaluation set of amyloid-ligand complexes constructed from model-generated poses validated by domain experts. Experiments on both experimentally resolved structures and this evaluation set demonstrate improved pose quality and binding affinity correlation over existing docking baselines.

</details>


### [138] [AGG: Jacobian-Aggregated Group Gradient for Efficient GRPO Training of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17572)
*Ruiyi Ding,Jie Li,He Kang,Ziyan Liu,Chengru Song,Yuan chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JAGG通过利用DiT隐藏状态沿轨迹的线性特性，将GRPO训练中完整的Transformer反向传播从W次减少到每组W步仅2次，在T2I任务上实现约2倍反向传播加速且质量几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO扩展到扩散/流匹配模型时，需要在采样轨迹的每个时间步对高容量DiT骨干网络进行反向传播梯度，导致高分辨率文生图训练的计算成本过高。

Method: 提出JAGG方法，利用DiT隐藏状态和速度预测沿轨迹平滑且近乎线性的特性：通过t加权的端点Jacobian插值近似中间步Jacobian，将多个时间步的上游信号聚合为两个复合梯度，通过单次联合反向传播应用。同时设计余弦相似度路由规则（jagg_frac）仅在线性假设成立时启用JAGG。

Result: 在T2I基准测试中，JAGG实现了约2倍的反向传播加速，且生成质量几乎无退化。

Conclusion: 证明了当速度在(z,t)上线性时插值精确成立，JAGG有效解决了DiT强化学习训练的计算瓶颈，为扩散模型的高效RL对齐提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is a powerful reinforcement learning algorithm for aligning generative models with human preferences. While successful in large language models~\cite{shao2024deepseekmathpushinglimitsmathematical}, its extension to diffusion and flow matching models introduces a severe computational bottleneck: gradients must be back-propagated through the high-capacity DiT backbone at \emph{every} timestep of the sampling trajectory, making high-resolution text-to-image (T2I) training prohibitively expensive. Training-free DiT inference acceleration methods (e.g., $Δ$-DiT, ScalingCache) exploit the fact that DiT hidden states and velocity predictions vary \emph{smoothly and nearly linearly} along the trajectory. We ask whether the same linearity can reduce the backward-pass cost of DiT RL training, and answer affirmatively with \textbf{JAGG} (\textbf{J}acobian-\textbf{A}ggregated \textbf{G}roup \textbf{G}radient), which reduces full transformer backward passes from $W$ to $2$ per group of $W$ consecutive steps. JAGG approximates intermediate-step Jacobians via $t$-weighted interpolation of the endpoint Jacobians, then aggregates per-step upstream signals into two composite gradients applied through a single joint backward pass. We prove this interpolation is \emph{exact} when the velocity is linear in $(z,t)$, and a cosine-similarity routing rule (\texttt{jagg\_frac}) deploys JAGG only where the assumption holds. Experiments on T2I benchmarks show JAGG delivers $\sim$2$\times$ backward speedup with negligible quality degradation.

</details>


### [139] [Concentration and Mean-Square Bounds for Contractive Stochastic Approximation: A Unified Elementary Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17595)
*Siddharth Chandak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对具有任意范数压缩映射的随机逼近算法，在乘性噪声（噪声可随迭代范数仿射缩放）且迭代可能无界的设定下，建立了均方界和浓度界。作者通过平均化噪声序列和辅助迭代变量，导出了范数误差的单步Lyapunov漂移不等式，无需对范数进行光滑化或构造Moreau包络。基于该不等式，通过归纳证明迭代在期望下有界，以及通过概率归纳构造"好事件"序列，分别获得了均方界和亚高斯尾部的最大（全程）浓度界。该方法统一且初等，并首次在乘性噪声下实现了步长对数依赖于置信度的亚高斯尾浓度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强化学习等应用中，算子常在ℓ_∞范数下压缩，噪声随迭代缩放，且迭代可能无界。现有方法需用广义Moreau包络构造光滑Lyapunov函数进行均方分析，并通过多阶段自举论证处理乘性噪声和无界迭代以获得浓度界。本文旨在提供一种统一、初等的分析方法，同时获得均方和浓度界，并首次在乘性噪声下导出亚高斯尾部的最优浓度界。

Method: 1) 引入平均化噪声序列和对应的辅助迭代变量，直接对范数误差建立单步Lyapunov漂移不等式，无需光滑化范数或构造Moreau包络；2) 对于均方界，结合漂移不等式与归纳论证，证明迭代在期望意义下有界；3) 对于浓度界，构建一个概率归纳框架，定义一系列"好事件"控制迭代行为，使得标准Azuma-Hoeffding不等式可被应用，并通过允许步长对置信水平对数依赖，获得亚高斯尾部最大浓度界。

Result: 1) 获得了乘性噪声下SA算法的均方收敛界；2) 首次获得了乘性噪声下SA算法的亚高斯尾部最大（全程）浓度界，且步长可对数依赖于置信水平；3) 提供了比现有多阶段自举论证更简单统一的证明框架。

Conclusion: 本文提出的统一分析方法有效替代了现有基于Moreau包络光滑化和多阶段自举论证的复杂方法，且所获浓度界在乘性噪声下达到了亚高斯尾部的最优形式。该证明技术可推广至其他噪声模型和迭代算法。

Abstract: We establish mean-square and concentration bounds for stochastic approximation (SA) with arbitrary norm contractive mappings, under a multiplicative noise model where the noise may scale affinely with the norm of the iterates, and the iterates are potentially unbounded. These settings arise in reinforcement learning, where operators are often contractive in the $\ell_\infty$ norm and the noise scales with the iterates. To address the arbitrary norm, earlier works replace the non-smooth squared norm with a smooth Lyapunov function constructed via the generalized Moreau envelope. For concentration analysis, these works handle multiplicative noise and unbounded iterates through a multi-stage bootstrapping argument that starts from a time-varying worst-case bound and iteratively refines it. We instead present a unified and elementary analysis that yields both bounds. Using an averaged noise sequence and corresponding auxiliary iterates, we obtain a one-step Lyapunov drift inequality for the normed error directly, without smoothing the norm or constructing an envelope. For the mean-square bound, we combine this drift inequality with an induction argument showing that the iterates remain bounded in expectation. For the concentration bound, we develop a probabilistic induction over a sequence of "good" events on which the iterates are controlled, allowing the standard Azuma-Hoeffding bound to be applied. Our approach yields the first sub-Gaussian tailed maximal (all-time) concentration bound for SA under multiplicative noise, by allowing the stepsize to depend logarithmically on the confidence level. Beyond the specific setting considered here, we discuss the generalizability of these proof techniques to other noise models and iterative algorithms.

</details>


### [140] [Planning with Transformers: Chain of Computation and Structured Context Windows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17710)
*Ehsan Futuhi,Nathan R. Sturtevant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Chain of Computation (COC)架构，通过在迭代循环中部署基于Transformer的语言模型，结合结构化上下文窗口（SCW），使LLM能够学习规划策略、预测世界模型并执行算术操作。实验表明，即使是从头训练的小规模LM，在BlocksWorld和Pancake puzzle等规划任务中也能达到99.89%以上的成功率，并能解决需要超过100万步操作的20盘汉诺塔问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在理论上是图灵完备的，具备强大的计算能力，但在实际规划任务中表现不佳。本文旨在弥合这一理论与实证表现之间的差距，探索如何通过合适的计算架构释放LLM在规划问题上的潜力。

Method: 提出COC架构，将基于Transformer的LM置于迭代循环中，利用其模式匹配能力。该架构包含结构化上下文窗口（SCW），支持在每个规划步骤选择使用哪个窗口。LM在架构内学习规划策略、预测世界模型并执行所需算术运算。通过仅追加型SCW（类似图灵机磁带）实现规划。

Result: 从头训练的小规模LM在BlocksWorld和Pancake puzzle上达到99.89%以上成功率。在汉诺塔任务中，通过符号算术支持或使用确定性下推自动机（PDA）形式的SCW，可解决最多20个盘（需超100万步动作）的问题实例，同时大幅降低训练数据需求。

Conclusion: 通过合理的计算架构设计（COC+SCW），即使小规模LM也能在复杂规划任务上取得卓越表现，弥合了LLM理论计算能力与实证规划性能之间的差距。失败案例分析表明，汉诺塔中的错误主要源于算术操作或未见过的标记，可通过符号化支持或PDA形式化解耦来解决。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have had a remarkable impact across many areas of machine learning. However, recent studies have shown that they struggle to reliably solve planning problems. At the same time, theoretical results have shown that transformers, the core architecture underlying modern LLMs, are Turing-complete. In this work, we investigate this apparent gap between the theoretical computational power of LLMs and their empirical planning performance. We propose Chain of Computation (COC), a computational architecture that places a transformer-based LM inside an iterative loop, leveraging its strength as a pattern-matching system. The COC uses a Structured Context Window (SCW) which provides a constant-sized context window with support for choosing which window is used at each planning step. Within this architecture, the LM is able to learn a planning policy, predicts the world model, and performs the arithmetic operations required during planning. We show that, when given an append-only SCW (resembling a Turing Machine tape), even relatively small LMs trained from scratch can learn planning policies and generalize from a small number of training instances within each planning domain, achieving success rates above 99.89\% on BlocksWorld and the Pancake puzzle. Our analysis of failure cases in Tower of Hanoi (TOH) reveals that they arise from arithmetic operations or from encountering previously unseen tokens. We show that COC can solve TOH problem instances with up to 20 disks, requiring over 1 million actions, while requiring substantially less training data by either (1) planning with symbolical support for arithmetic or by (2) using a deterministic pushdown automaton (PDA) formulation for the SCW.

</details>


### [141] [Theoretical Foundations of $\max$@$k$ Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17823)
*Riccardo Poiani,Martino Bernasconi,Andrea Celli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究了有限时域强化学习中的max@k学习问题，证明其与标准期望回报最大化存在本质差异：马尔可夫策略通常不足以达到最优，需通过状态增广恢复最优性，且学习max@k最优策略的统计难度高于标准强化学习，同时给出了达到最优样本复杂度的有效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管max@k指标在实际应用（如代码生成、定理证明）中广泛使用，但其理论基础尚不充分；现有强化学习理论主要针对期望回报最大化，而max@k优化问题具有独特性质，需要专门的理论分析。

Method: 本文采用有限时域强化学习框架，通过理论分析刻画max@k目标与标准期望回报最大化的差异；证明马尔可夫策略的不足并构造状态增广方法恢复最优性；分析策略类之间的性能差距；推导统计复杂度下界并设计达到最优样本效率的算法。

Result: 证明了马尔可夫策略对max@k优化通常不充分；找到了能恢复最优性的紧凑状态增广方案；显式刻画了历史依赖策略与非历史依赖策略间的性能差距；证明了学习max@k最优策略的统计难度更高；给出了达到最优样本复杂度率的有效算法。

Conclusion: max@k学习问题在理论上与标准强化学习有本质区别，需要状态增广和历史依赖策略；该问题的统计学习更具挑战性，但存在样本最优的高效算法。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning is a cornerstone technique for modern large reasoning models. Usually, for difficult tasks such as code generation and theorem proving, the agent is evaluated by generating $K$ responses rather than sampling a single response, and performance is then measured using a retry-aware metric such as $\max$@$k$. Despite their practical importance, the theoretical foundations of learning under such criteria remain limited. In this work, we provide a theoretical study of the $\max$@$k$ learning problem in finite-horizon reinforcement learning. We show that optimizing the $\max$@$k$ objectives is fundamentally different from standard expected-return maximization. In particular, we prove that Markovian policies are in general insufficient, identify a compact state augmentation that restores optimality, and explicitly characterize the performance gap that can arise between history-dependent and non-history-dependent policies. Moreover, we show that learning $\max$@$k$-optimal policies is statistically harder than standard reinforcement learning and provide an efficient algorithm that achieves the optimal sample complexity rate.

</details>


### [142] [Distributional Soft Bellman Operator under the Cramér Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.17897)
*Keru Wang,Yixin Deng,Yao Lyu,Stephen Redmond,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在Cramér几何（基于CDF的L²度量）下，分布性软策略迭代（DSPI）中的固定策略分布性软贝尔曼算子具有√γ-压缩性，从而存在唯一不动点，并给出了收敛的迭代策略评估。该性质只需对组合的一步奖励熵移位施加一致一阶矩条件，而非对奖励和熵项分别施加一致有界性假设。进一步通过共轭变换将评估问题转化到谱域，得到等价的Hilbert空间表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布性软策略迭代（DSPI）结合了分布性强化学习（DRL）与最大熵控制，其策略评估步骤由作用于熵正则化回报的分布性软贝尔曼算子控制。对该评估步骤进行理论分析需要一个概率度量来控制贝尔曼更新，通常通过证明该算子压缩任意两个候选回报分布估计之间的距离来实现。本文聚焦于Cramér几何（基于CDF的L²度量），研究固定策略的分布性软贝尔曼算子在该度量下是否具有压缩性及唯一不动点。

Method: 直接在可容许的CDF域上工作，构建CDF层面的分布性软贝尔曼算子，证明其为√γ-压缩并获得唯一不动点及收敛的迭代策略评估。CDF公式表明该有限Cramér域性质源于对组合的一步奖励熵移位的一致一阶矩条件。进一步通过共轭变换将评估问题转化到谱域，得到等价的Hilbert空间表示。

Result: 证明了CDF层面的分布性软贝尔曼算子是√γ-压缩映射，存在唯一不动点，且迭代策略评估收敛。该性质不依赖对奖励和熵项的分别有界性假设，而只需要对组合的一步奖励熵移位的一致一阶矩条件。谱域变换获得了等价的Hilbert空间表示。

Conclusion: 确定了DSPI策略评估步骤在Cramér几何下的贝尔曼不动点，为研究DSPI类算法中的近似评论家、评估误差和评论家损失设计提供了参考依据。
